def convolve_sq_fft(a, fftsize=None):
    size = 1 << (2*len(a)-1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*len(a)-1]
    return c
```

Compute r_i = ∑_j a_j q_{i+j} = conv(a, q_rev)[Lq-1 - i] (0-index). Let's implement:
```
def correlate_q_a_fft(q,a):
    n=a.size; Lq=q.size
    size=1 << (n+Lq-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    fq=np.fft.rfft(q[::-1],size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fq,size)[:n+Lq-1]
    idx = Lq-1 - np.arange(n)
    return conv[idx]
```

Implement functions top-level:
```
def soft_objective_grad(z, tau, S, fft_size=None):
    # z 1d
    zm = z - np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(zm)
    p = ez / ez.sum()
    a = S * p
    c = conv_sq_fft(a)
    cm = c.max()
    ex = np.exp((c-cm)/tau)
    q = ex / ex.sum()
    F = cm + tau*np.log(ex.sum()) # tau*...
    r = correlate_q_a_fft(q,a)
    grad_a = 2.0*r
    grad_z = a * (grad_a - np.dot(a, grad_a))
    return F, grad_z
```
Need ensure `np.dot(a, grad_a)` O(n). Good.

In `_fun_and_grad(x, tau, S, N, n)`:
Number of args could be (tau,S,N,n). Use top-level.
```
def _fun_and_grad(z, tau, S, N, n):
    # try compute
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    zm = z - np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(zm)
    ez_sum = ez.sum()
    if ez_sum <=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    p=ez/ez_sum
    a=S*p
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    cm = c.max()
    # if tau <=0? use exact max? We won't.
    shift = c - cm
    # If tau very small and shift/tau has large exponent for max 0; for negative large underflow ok.
    ex = np.exp(shift/tau)
    ex_sum = ex.sum()
    if ex_sum <=0:
       q = np.zeros(2*n-1); q[argmax]=1
    else: q=ex/ex_sum
    val = cm + tau*np.log(ex_sum)
    # gradient correlation
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fq, N)
    r = r[:n]
    grad_a = 2.0*r
    ga_dot_a = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_z = a * (grad_a - ga_dot_a)
    # clean tiny
    grad_z[~np.isfinite(grad_z)] = 0
    if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100, zeros
    return float(val), grad_z
```
Check `np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fq, N)`: `fa` is rfft real a, `fq` rfft q. Multiplication complex length N//2+1. OK.

Potential issue: `val` with S scaling: c computed with a sum S. q weights. If tau small, val~max c. Good.

But L-BFGS objective value has arbitrary offset; line search wants decrease. Good.

def _run_lbfgs_for_tau(a0 or z0, tau, S,N,n, budget, z=current):
   # loop chunks

      if res.status? maybe break after score not improved for consecutive.
```

Let's attempt a low-dimensional optimization of a parametric family:
a_i = x_i^{-0.5} * exp(∑_{k=1}^K θ_k T_k(x_i)) / norm, with x_i=(i+0.5)/n. Basis T_k maybe Chebyshev polynomials on [0,1] to represent modulation. K=8. We can implement:
- K=8 basis: Chebyshev (shifted) T_k(2x-1) maybe, but ensure no singular at endpoints besides x^-0.5. Chebyshev can change sign, exp keeps positive.
- Param vector θ, length K. Use fixed n=2000 or 3000 for search to be fast. Objective = soft tau logsumexp(c/tau) with sum S=√(2n) (score=maxc). But for continuous optimum, n=2000 enough shape. Use tau maybe 0.05.
- Use scipy L-BFGS on θ (small) with objective computing a, conv FFT, soft max; gradient wrt θ: da/dθ = a * (φ_k - ∑ p φ_k). Similar softmax logic with multiple features. Then L-BFGS. This is fast (n=2000). We can do many random restarts in θ. This might find shape with score <1.52 even for n=2000 finite. Then upsample.

This is a clever approach; likely improve. Let's design details.

Given features Φ (n,K). Let w(x)=exp(θ·Φ). a = x^{-0.5} * w / norm. Actually sum fixed S. For gradient wrt θ, similar to softmax in log a: log a_i = -0.5 log x_i + θ·Φ_i - log Z. The parameter θ is coefficient. Gradient wrt θ_j = a_j (grad_a_j - ∑ a_i grad_a_i) (same as softmax feature gradient). Because ∂log a_i/∂θ_j=Φ_ij (Fisher information with offset). Indeed a = S*exp(v)/Z, v=-0.5logx + Φθ. ∂a_i/∂θ_j = a_i(Φ_ij - mean_a Φ). So gradθ = (Φ - Φ_mean) * a * (grad_a - dot). More explicitly: inner = grad_a - dot(a,grad_a); gradθ = Φ.T @ (a * inner). This is O(nK). Nice.

This parametric search can be very fast. Use n=2000/3000, K=10. Then output high resolution n_main by evaluating with θ best: a_i = x_i^{-0.5} exp(Φ(x_i)θ) normalize. Note x_i>0. For i=0, x=1/(2n), finite.

The offset x^{-0.5} captures endpoint singular. If optimal doesn't have singular exponent 0.5? Could be. We can include exponent α as parameter: a_i ∝ x_i^{-α} exp(θ·Φ). α in [0.3,0.6]? If α parameter, gradient wrt α = a * (-log x - mean...) * inner. Add as parameter automatically fixed scale? Let parameters = [α, θ1..θK]. x=-log? v_i = -α log x_i + θ·Φ_i. We can optimize α too but need constrain; L-BFGS-B with bounds [0,1]. This could find optimal singular exponent. Use bounds for α [0.0,0.75] maybe. But if α>0.5 discrete endpoint may high; but shape search will see.

This is promising! Low-dimensional global shape + high-dimensional polish.

We can also include a parameter for endpoint mass (a0) separate? Discrete boundary atom not captured by continuous x^-α? We can allow modifying first few entries? High-dim polish can.

Implement parametric objective:
```
def _param_segments(n, K):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   base=-0.5*np.log(x)
   # features shifted Chebyshev 1..K
   t=2*x-1
   T=np.zeros((n,K))
   T[:,0]=t
   if K>1: T[:,1]=2*t*t-1
   for k 2..K-1: T[:,k]=2*t*T[:,k-1]-T[:,k-2]
   return x, base, T
```
Include constant? If α captures scale, constant not needed (normalization removes). But to allow overall shape near0 shift, α can vary; constant in θ redundant. Use K features 1..K.

Param vector p = [alpha, theta...]. Bounds: alpha [0.0,0.8], theta [-3,3] maybe. During eval, if alpha out? L-BFGS-B enforces.

Objective and gradient:
```
def _param_fun_grad(p, n, S, data, tau):
   alpha=p[0]; theta=p[1:]
   v = -alpha*data['logx'] + data['T'].dot(theta)
   # stable softmax
   psoftmax... a=S*exp(v)/Z
   c=conv fft (precompute N, n)
   q=softmax(c/tau)
   r=correlate fft
   grad_a=2*r
   inner = grad_a - dot(a,grad_a)
   inner_a = a*inner
   grad_alpha = -dot(inner_a, logx?) Wait v_i = -α log x_i + Tθ. ∂v_i/∂α = -logx_i. So grad_α = ∑ a_i(inner)_i * (-logx_i) = -dot(inner_a, logx).
   grad_theta = T.T @ inner_a
   return val, [grad_alpha, grad_theta...]
```
Need pass logx=data['logx'].

In `minimize`, args include tau,S,N,n,base? `data` dict as arg. Good.

This low-dim objective can be called many times. We can run multi-start:

Let's implement `_conv_square_fft(a)` returning c only.

```
def _conv_square_fft(a):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=_fft_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
    return c
```

Helper functions:

`_fft_pow2(m)`.
`_softmax_fract(x, S)`: stable. Use in many.
```
def _softmax_fract(z, S):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not all finite: return None
    z = z - np.max(z)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None
    return S*ez/s
```
`_z_from_a(a)`:
```
def _z_from_a(a, eps_rel=1e-12):
    amin = a.max()*eps_rel
    if amin <=0: amin=1e-300
    return np.log(np.maximum(a, amin))
```

`_budget_stop`? Use param.

`_quick_score(a)`:
```
def _quick_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    if not finite: return inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-6: return inf
    c=_conv_square_fft(a)
    mc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    n=a.size
    return float(2*n*mc/(s*s))
```
For n small, FFT fine. If a has negative, clip; our a positive.

`_update_best(best_a,best_sc,a,verbose,name='')` top-level returns. It computes score via `_quick_score`. Print if improve. But frequent prints? Fine.

def _update_best(best_a,best_sc,a,name=''):
   if a is None or len==0 return best
   sc=_quick_score(a)
   if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
      if verbose print
      return a.copy(), sc
   return best
```

Param data:
```
def _make_param_data(n, K):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    neglog = -np.log(x)
    T=np.empty((n,K))
    if K>0:
       t=2*x-1
       T[:,0]=t
       if K>1:
          T[:,1]=2*t*t-1
          for k in range(2,K): T[:,k]=2*t*T[:,k-1]-T[:,k-2]
    return neglog, T
```
If K=1, T defined. If n large, T memory n*K; for n=7500,K=12 ->720k floats ~5.8MB, fine. We won't keep for high-dim.

`_param_shape(n,K,p,S)`:
```
neglog,T=_make_param_data(n,K)
alpha=p[0]; theta=p[1:1+K]
v = -alpha*neglog
if K: v += T @ theta
return _softmax_fract(v,S)
```
Could avoid remaking T if just optimized at same n; pass data. Use `_param_shape_from_data`.

Param fun:
```
def _param_fun_grad(p, tau, n, S, N, neglog, T):
   a = ... using data
   fa... c... val, q...
   grad_a...
   inner_a = a*(grad_a - dot)
   grad = np.empty(len(p))
   grad[0] = -np.dot(inner_a, neglog)
   if T.size: grad[1:] = T.T @ inner_a
   return val, grad
```
Need p length maybe K+1. If `minimize` within bounds, p finite. If not, handle.

Param search:
```
def _param_search(n, K, budget, stop_time, verbose=True):
    start_time=time.monotonic()
    neglog,T=_make_param_data(n,K)
    S=np.sqrt(2*n); N=_fft_pow2(2*n-1)
    bounds=[(0.0,0.8)] + [(-5,5)]*K
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    starts=[]
    base=np.zeros(K+1); base[0]=0.5
    starts.append(base.copy())
    for i in range(15):
       p=base.copy()
       p[0]=0.35+0.3*rng.random()
       p[1:]=rng.normal(0,0.7,K)

             if res.x is not None and finite: p=res.x
           except Exception: pass
           # evaluate

       # optionally if time, add perturbed best
    return best_p,best_a,best_sc

def _run_lbfgs_seq(a0, budget, stop_time, tau_schedule=None, verbose=False):
   a=np.asarray(a0,float).ravel(); if not finite return
   n=a.size; S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=_normalize_sum(a,S)
   best=a; best_sc=_score_from_conv(a, c)
   z=_z_from_a(a); z-=z.mean()
   N=_fft_pow2(2*n-1)
   if tau_schedule is None: tau_schedule=[(0.3,0.15),(0.1,0.25),(0.04,0.25),(0.015,0.2),(0.005,0.15)]
   t_end=min(stop_time, time+budget)
   for tau,frac in tau_schedule:
      tau_budget=frac*budget
      tau_end=min(t_end, time+tau_budget)
      while time<tau_end-2:
         chunk=10 if n>6000 else 20
         try: res=minimize(_fun_and_grad,z,method='L-BFGS-B',args=(tau,S,N,n), options={'maxiter':chunk,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxfun':5*chunk})
         except: break
         if res.x finite: z=res.x; z-=z.mean()
         a=_softmax_fract(z,S)
         if a is None: break
         sc=_score_from_conv(a, _conv_square_fft(a))
         if sc<best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
         if time>t_end: break
         # if improve small? continue
   return best,best_sc

Let's implement `_safe_score(a, c=None)`:
```
def _safe_score(a, c=None):
   a=np.asarray(a)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   if not finite: return inf
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s <=0: return inf
   if c is None: c=_conv_square_fft(a)
   mc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   if mc<0: mc=0.0
   return float(2*a.size*mc/(s*s))
```

Now, if a has values small and sum S, score from c maybe 1.5. Good.

```
def _param_search(n, K, budget, stop_time, verbose=True):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    neglog,T = _make_param_data(n,K)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0*n); N = _fft_pow2(2*n-1)
    bounds = [(0.0,0.8)]
    for k in range(K): bounds.append((-5.0,5.0))
    starts=[]
    base=np.zeros(K+1); base[0]=0.5
    starts.append(base)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(20240501)
    for i in range(12):
       p=base.copy()
       p[0]=0.35 + 0.3*rng.random()
       p[1:]=rng.normal(0.0,0.7,K)
       p=_clip_params(p,bounds)
       starts.append(p)
    best_p=base.copy(); best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    tau_list=[0.5,0.2,0.08,0.03,0.01,0.004]
    for p0 in starts:
       if time.monotonic() > stop_time or time.monotonic() - t0 > budget: break
       p=p0.copy()
       for tau in tau_list:
          if time.monotonic() > stop_time or time.monotonic() - t0 > budget: break
          try:
             res=minimize(_param_fun_grad, p, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, args=(tau,n,S,N,neglog,T), options={'maxiter':300,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
             if res.x is not None:
                px=np.asarray(res.x,float)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(px)): p=px
          except Exception:
             pass
          a=_param_shape_from_data(n,S,p,neglog,T)
          if a is not None:
             c=_conv_square_fft(a); sc=_score_from_conv(a,c)
             if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
                if verbose: print(f"    param n={n} score={sc:.6f}")
       # if time, optionally add perturbed best
    return best_p, best_a, best_sc
```

In `_run_lbfgs_seq`, `budget` may be less than max? We pass positive. Use tau_schedule fractions of `budget`. If `budget` too small, skip.

```
def _run_lbfgs_seq(a0, budget, stop_time, tau_schedule=None, verbose=True, name=''):
    rem = min(budget, stop_time - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 5: return None,np.inf
    t_end = time.monotonic() + rem
    a = np.asarray(a0, float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None,inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    if not finite or sum<1e-6: return None,inf
    n=a.size; S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=_normalize_sum(a,S)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_safe_score(a)
    z=_z_from_a(a); z-=z.mean()
    N=_fft_pow2(2*n-1)
    if tau_schedule is None: tau_schedule=[(0.3,0.15),(0.12,0.25),(0.05,0.25),(0.02,0.15),(0.008,0.1),(0.003,0.1)]
    if verbose: print(f"  LBFGS {name}: n={n} initial={best_sc:.6f} budget={rem:.0f}s")
    for idx,(tau,frac) in enumerate(tau_schedule):
       if time.monotonic() >= t_end: break
       tau_end = min(t_end, time.monotonic() + frac*rem)
       chunk = 8 if n > 6000 else (15 if n > 2500 else 30)
       no_improve=0
       while time.monotonic() < tau_end - 2:
          try:
             res = minimize(_fun_and_grad, z, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(tau,S,N,n), options={'maxiter':chunk,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxfun':10*chunk})
          except Exception:
             break
          if res.x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): break
          z = np.asarray(res.x,float)
          z -= np.mean(z)
          a = _softmax_fract(z,S)
          if a is None: break
          sc = _safe_score(a)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; no_improve=0
             if verbose: print(f"    {name} tau={tau} score={sc:.6f}")
          else:
             no_improve += 1
             if no_improve >= 4 and time.monotonic() > tau_end*0? break? Maybe break tau if no progress.
          if res.status in (0,1) and res.nit < chunk:
             # converged for this tau; break to next tau
             break
       # after tau, maybe evaluate
    return best,best_sc
```
Need not use `tau_end*0`. If no_improve breaks too early? We can break tau after 3 no-improve chunks to save. But if it will improve later even without improvement at this tau? Unlikely. Let's break after 3 no-improve chunks.
But `no_improve` increments per chunk if score didn't improve; if chunk small, 3 maybe enough. Use 4.

Potential issue: `res.status` values: if maxiter reached, status 2? If success, status 1 maybe. If success and nit<chunk, break. Good.

The line search may fail and res.x not updated; if score not improve, no_improve.

Implement final polish in `z` or `a`? Use `a` direct with projection onto simplex. We have `_project_simplex`. Let's implement top-level:
```
def _project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
    s=v.sum()
    n=v.size
    if s <= S:
        return v + (S-s)/n
    lo=0.0; hi=float(v.max())
    for _ in range(40):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       if float(np.sum(np.maximum(v-mid,0.0))) > S:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    t=(lo+hi)/2
    res=np.maximum(v-t,0.0)
    diff=S-res.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(res))
       res[idx]+=diff
    return res
```

      c=conv; M=max
      active = c > M - max(1e-4*M, 1e-8)
      q=active.sum? use q=active/active.sum

Let's draft final code fully in analysis and check syntax.

```
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _fft_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _conv_square_fft(a):
    n = a.size
    L = 2*n - 1
    N = _fft_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c[:L]

def _score_from_conv(a, c):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
       return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
       return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-8:
       return np.inf
    if c is None:
       c = _conv_square_fft(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if mc < 0.0: mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mc / (s*s))

def _quick_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if np.sum(a)<=1e-8: return np.inf
    c=_conv_square_fft(a)
    return _score_from_conv(a,c)
```

Normalize:
```
def _normalize_sum(a,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   s=sum(a)
   if s<=0 or not finite: return a
   return a*(S/s)
```

Softmax:
```
def _softmax_fract(v,S):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   if v.size==0 or not finite: return None
   v = v - np.max(v)
   ev=np.exp(v)
   s=ev.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return None
   return S*ev/s
```

z_from_a:
```
def _z_from_a(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   amax=float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 1.0
   eps=amax*1e-12
   if eps<=0 or not finite: eps=1e-300
   return np.log(np.maximum(a, eps))
```

Update best:
```
def _update_best(best_a,best_sc,a,name=''):
   if a is None: return best_a,best_sc
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return...
   sc=_quick_score(a)
   if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
       print(f"  new best {name}: {sc:.6f}")
       return a.copy(), sc
   return best_a,best_sc
```

Candidate maker:
```
def _make_candidate(a,label,idx,min_size=500,max_size=10000):
   if a is None: return None
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size<min_size or a.size>max_size: return None
   if not finite or sum<=0: return None
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   sc=_quick_score(a)
   if not finite: return None
   return (sc, idx, label, a)
```

Interp:
```
def _interp_seq(seq,n):
   seq=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
   if seq.size==n: return seq.copy()
   if seq.size==0: return None
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,seq.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   return np.maximum(np.interp(x_new,x_old,seq),0.0)
```

Param data:
```
def _make_param_data(n,K):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   neglog = -np.log(x)
   T=np.empty((n,K), dtype=float)
   if K>0:
      t=2*x-1
      T[:,0]=t
      if K>1:
         T[:,1]=2*t*t-1
         for k in range(2,K):
             T[:,k]=2*t*T[:,k-1]-T[:,k-2]
   return neglog, T
```
If K=0, T uninitialized but not used; we use K=10.

Param shape:
```
def _param_shape_from_data(n,S,p,neglog,T):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   v = -p[0]*neglog
   if T.size and p.size > max(1,T.shape[1]):
      v = v + T @ p[1:1+T.shape[1]]
   return _softmax_fract(v,S)
```
If p length maybe K+1. Good.

Param shape:
```
def _param_shape(n,K,p,S):
   neglog,T=_make_param_data(n,K)
   return _param_shape_from_data(n,S,p,neglog,T)
```

Param fun:
```
def _param_fun_grad(p, tau, n, S, N, neglog, T):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   if not finite: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(p)
   v=-p[0]*neglog
   K=T.shape[1] if T.size else 0
   if K:
      v = v + T @ p[1:1+K]
   a=_softmax_fract(v,S)
   if a is None: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(p)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
   cm=float(np.max(c))
   shift=c-cm
   ex=np.exp(shift/tau)
   s=ex.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
   q=ex/s
   val=cm + tau*np.log(s)
   fq=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
   r=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fq,N)[:n]
   grad_a=2*r
   w=float(np.dot(a/S, grad_a))
   diff=grad_a - w
   adiff=a*diff
   grad=np.empty_like(p)
   grad[0] = -float(np.dot(adiff, neglog))
   if K:
      grad[1:1+K] = T.T @ adiff
   grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
   if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100, zeros
   return float(val), grad
```
Need if p shorter than K+1? In minimize p length K+1. Good.

Param search:
```
def _clip_params(p,bounds):
   out=np.empty_like(p)
   for i, val in enumerate(p):
      lo,hi=bounds[i]
      if not np.isfinite(val): val=0.0
      if val<lo: val=lo
      if val>hi: val=hi
      out[i]=val
   return out

def _param_search(n,K,budget,stop_time,verbose=True):
   t0=time.monotonic()
   neglog,T=_make_param_data(n,K)
   S=np.sqrt(2.0*n); N=_fft_pow2(2*n-1)
   bounds=[(0.0,0.8)]
   for k in range(K): bounds.append((-5.0,5.0))
   rng=np.random.default_rng(1234567)
   starts=[]
   base=np.zeros(K+1); base[0]=0.5
   starts.append(base)
   for _ in range(9):
      p=base.copy()
      p[0]=0.35 + 0.3*rng.random()
      p[1:]=rng.normal(0.0,0.7,K)
      starts.append(_clip_params(p,bounds))
   best_p=base.copy(); best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
   tau_list=[0.5,0.2,0.08,0.03,0.01,0.004]
   for p0 in starts:
      if time.monotonic()>stop_time or time.monotonic()-t0 > budget: break
      p=p0.copy()
      for tau in tau_list:
         if time.monotonic()>stop_time or time.monotonic()-t0 > budget: break
         try:
            res=minimize(_param_fun_grad, p, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, args=(tau,n,S,N,neglog,T), options={'maxiter':200,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
            if res.x is not None:
               px=np.asarray(res.x,float)
               if px.size==p.size and np.all(np.isfinite(px)): p=px
         except Exception:
            pass
         a=_param_shape_from_data(n,S,p,neglog,T)
         if a is not None:
            c=_conv_square_fft(a); sc=_score_from_conv(a,c)
            if sc < best_sc:
               best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
               if verbose:
                   print(f"    param n={n} score={sc:.6f}")
   return best_p, best_a, best_sc
```
If budget small, base p returned.

High fun:
```
def _fun_and_grad(z, tau, S, N, n):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size != n or not finite: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    v=z - np.max(z)
    ev=np.exp(v); evs=ev.sum()
    if evs<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    a=S*ev/evs
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
    cm=float(np.max(c))
    ex=np.exp((c-cm)/tau); s=ex.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       q=np.zeros(2*n-1); amax=int(np.argmax(c)); q[amax]=1.0; s=1.0
    else: q=ex/s
    val=cm + tau*np.log(s)
    fq=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fq,N)[:n]
    grad_a=2*r
    w=float(np.dot(a/S, grad_a))
    grad_z=a*(grad_a - w)
    grad_z[~finite]=0
    if not finite val: return 1e100 zeros
    return float(val), grad_z
```

status_success = False
try:
   if res.x is not None:
      z = ...; a=...; sc=...; update
      if int(res.status) == 0 or int(res.status) == 1:
          # likely converged
          break
except Exception: break
```

Actually `L-BFGS-B` statuses: 0: Converged, 1: Converged (same?), 2: Maxiter, 7: Rounding error? Wait. Use `if res.status in (0,1): break`. If maxiter reached, status 2, continue. Good.

Time within while: `while time.monotonic() < tau_end - 2.0:`. If a minimize chunk exceeds tau_end significantly, we still after it check. OK.

```
def _run_lbfgs_seq(a0,budget,stop_time,tau_schedule=None,verbose=True,name=''):
   rem = min(budget, stop_time-time.monotonic())
   if rem < 10: return None, np.inf
   t_end=time.monotonic()+rem
   a=np.asarray(a0,float).ravel()
   if a.size<100 or not finite: return None,inf
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=float(np.sum(a)); if s<=0: return None,inf
   n=a.size; S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=a*(S/s)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_from_conv(a, _conv_square_fft(a))
   z=_z_from_a(a); z-=np.mean(z)
   N=_fft_pow2(2*n-1)
   if tau_schedule is None: ...
   if verbose: print(...)
   for tau,frac in tau_schedule:
      rem_now=min(t_end-time.monotonic(), stop_time-time.monotonic())
      if rem_now<5: break
      tau_end=min(t_end, time.monotonic()+frac*rem)
      chunk=8 if n>6000 else (15 if n>2500 else 30)
      no_improve=0
      while time.monotonic() < tau_end - 2.0:
         try:
            res=minimize(_fun_and_grad, z, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(tau,S,N,n), options={'maxiter':chunk,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxfun':10*chunk})
         except Exception:
            break
         if res.x is None or not finite: break
         z=np.asarray(res.x,float); z-=np.mean(z)
         a=_softmax_fract(z,S)
         if a is None: break
         c=_conv_square_fft(a); sc=_score_from_conv(a,c)
         if sc < best_sc -1e-12:
            best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; no_improve=0
            if verbose: print(f"    {name} tau={tau:.4f} score={sc:.6f}")
         else:
            no_improve +=1
         if int(res.status) in (0,1) or no_improve >= 3:
            break
   if verbose: print(f"  {name} final score={best_sc:.6f}")
   return best,best_sc
```
Potential issue: If no_improve increments when score not improved due numeric but res.status maxiter, after 3 chunks break. Good.

But what if the first tau has no improvement but later same tau would after longer? Break at 3 chunks maybe too early; but lower tau might help. OK.

Top-level `_add_final`:
```
def _add_final(cands,a):
   if a is None: return cands
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size>=100 and a.size<=10000 and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
      cands.append(a.copy())
   return cands
```

Now, initialization functions for random/chirp:
```
def _init_chirp(n, seed):
   t=np.arange(n,dtype=float) - (n-1)*0.5
   a=1.0 + 0.9*np.cos(np.pi*t*t/n)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   a=a + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
   return np.maximum(a,0.0)

def _init_random(n, seed):
   return np.random.default_rng(seed).exponential(1.0,size=n)
```

```python
"""
Search for low-maximum self-convolution step functions.

The objective is
    score = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
for nonnegative sequences a.

The program combines:
  1) a low-dimensional parametric model a_i ~ x_i^{-alpha} exp(theta.T phi_i)
     optimized by L-BFGS-B with annealed soft-max convexification;
  2) full-dimensional softmax-parameterized L-BFGS-B polishing;
  3) multiple initializations and exact finite-block final evaluation.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _fft_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _conv_square_fft(a):
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _fft_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c[:L]


def _score_from_conv(a, c):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-8:
        return np.inf
    if c is None:
        c = _conv_square_fft(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mc / (s * s))


def _quick_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 1e-8:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_square_fft(a)
    return _score_from_conv(a, c)


def _normalize_sum(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return a
    return a * (S / s)


def _softmax_fract(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return None
    v = v - np.max(v)
    ev = np.exp(v)
    s = float(np.sum(ev))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return (S / s) * ev


def _z_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    amax = float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 1.0
    eps = amax * 1e-12
    if not np.isfinite(eps) or eps <= 1e-300:
        eps = 1e-300
    return np.log(np.maximum(a, eps))


def _update_best(best_a, best_sc, a, name=""):
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_sc
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_a, best_sc
    sc = _quick_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
        print(f"  new best {name}: {sc:.6f}")
        return a.copy(), sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def _add_final(cands, a):
    if a is None:
        return cands
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if 100 <= a.size <= 10000 and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        cands.append(a.copy())
    return cands


def _refine_key(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    return (
        int(a.size),
        round(float(np.sum(a)), 12),
        round(float(a[0]), 12),
        round(float(a[-1]), 12),
    )


def _interp_seq(seq, n):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return None
    if seq.size == n:
        return seq.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.maximum(np.interp(x_new, x_old, seq), 0.0)


def _make_param_data(n, K):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    neglog = -np.log(x)
    T = np.empty((n, K), dtype=np.float64)
    if K > 0:
        t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
        T[:, 0] = t
        if K > 1:
            T[:, 1] = 2.0 * t * t - 1.0
            for k in range(2, K):
                T[:, k] = 2.0 * t * T[:, k - 1] - T[:, k - 2]
    return neglog, T


def _param_shape_from_data(n, S, p, neglog, T):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    Kmax = T.shape[1] if T.size else 0
    m = min(Kmax, p.size - 1)
    v = -p[0] * neglog
    if m > 0:
        v = v + T[:, :m] @ p[1:1 + m]
    return _softmax_fract(v, S)


def _param_shape(n, K, p, S):
    neglog, T = _make_param_data(n, K)
    return _param_shape_from_data(n, S, p, neglog, T)


def _param_fun_grad(p, tau, n, S, N, neglog, T):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(p, dtype=np.float64)

    K = T.shape[1] if T.size else 0
    m = min(K, p.size - 1)
    v = -p[0] * neglog
    if m > 0:
        v = v + T[:, :m] @ p[1:1 + m]

    a = _softmax_fract(v, S)
    if a is None:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(p, dtype=np.float64)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    ex = np.exp((c - cm) / tau)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        q = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        q = ex / s

    val = cm + tau * np.log(s)

    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fq, N)[:n]
    grad_a = 2.0 * r
    w = float(np.dot(a / S, grad_a))
    adiff = a * (grad_a - w)

    grad = np.zeros_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
    grad[0] = -float(np.dot(adiff, neglog))
    if m > 0:
        grad[1:1 + m] = T[:, :m].T @ adiff

    grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def _clip_params(p, bounds):
    out = np.empty_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
    for i, val in enumerate(p):
        lo, hi = bounds[i]
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            val = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if val < lo:
            val = lo
        elif val > hi:
            val = hi
        out[i] = val
    return out


def _param_search(n, K, budget, stop_time, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    neglog, T = _make_param_data(n, K)
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    N = _fft_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    bounds = [(0.0, 0.8)]
    for _ in range(K):
        bounds.append((-5.0, 5.0))

    base = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    base[0] = 0.5

    starts = [base.copy()]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1234567)
    for _ in range(9):
        p = base.copy()
        p[0] = 0.35 + 0.30 * rng.random()
        p[1:] = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, K)
        starts.append(_clip_params(p, bounds))

    best_p = base.copy()
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    tau_list = [0.5, 0.2, 0.08, 0.03, 0.01, 0.004]

    for p0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time or time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
            break
        p = p0.copy()
        for tau in tau_list:
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time or time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _param_fun_grad,
                    p,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    args=(tau, n, S, N, neglog, T),
                    options={"maxiter": 220, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8},
                )
                if res.x is not None:
                    px = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                    if px.size == p.size and np.all(np.isfinite(px)):
                        p = px
            except Exception:
                pass

            a = _param_shape_from_data(n, S, p, neglog, T)
            if a is not None:
                c = _conv_square_fft(a)
                sc = _score_from_conv(a, c)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    if verbose:
                        print(f"    param n={n} score={sc:.6f}")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _param_shape_from_data(n, S, best_p, neglog, T)

    return best_p, best_a, float(best_sc)


def _fun_and_grad(z, tau, S, N, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    v = z - np.max(z)
    ev = np.exp(v)
    s = float(np.sum(ev))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = S * ev / s

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    ex = np.exp((c - cm) / tau)
    s2 = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 1e-300:
        q = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s2 = 1.0
    else:
        q = ex / s2

    val = cm + tau * np.log(s2)

    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fq, N)[:n]
    grad_a = 2.0 * r
    w = float(np.dot(a / S, grad_a))
    gz = a * (grad_a - w)
    gz[~np.isfinite(gz)] = 0.0

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), gz


def _run_lbfgs_seq(a0, budget, stop_time, tau_schedule=None, verbose=True, name=""):
    rem = min(float(budget), stop_time - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 15.0:
        return None, np.inf

    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size < 100 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None, np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 1e-8:
        return None, np.inf

    n = a.size
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = _normalize_sum(a, S)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_from_conv(a, _conv_square_fft(a))

    z = _z_from_a(a)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    N = _fft_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    if tau_schedule is None:
        tau_schedule = [
            (0.3, 0.12),
            (0.12, 0.20),
            (0.05, 0.22),
            (0.02, 0.18),
            (0.008, 0.15),
            (0.003, 0.13),
        ]

    t_end = min(time.monotonic() + rem, stop_time)
    if verbose:
        print(f"  LBFGS {name}: n={n}, initial score={best_sc:.6f}, budget={rem:.0f}s")

    chunk = 8 if n > 6000 else (14 if n > 2500 else 25)

    for tau, frac in tau_schedule:
        if time.monotonic() >= t_end - 5.0:
            break
        tau_end = min(t_end, time.monotonic() + frac * rem)
        no_improve = 0
        while time.monotonic() < tau_end - 2.0:
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _fun_and_grad,
                    z,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    args=(tau, S, N, n),
                    options={
                        "maxiter": chunk,
                        "ftol": 1e-15,
                        "gtol": 1e-10,
                        "maxfun": max(20, 10 * chunk),
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            if res.x is None:
                break
            nz = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if nz.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(nz)):
                break
            nz -= float(np.mean(nz))
            z = nz

            na = _softmax_fract(z, S)
            if na is None:
                break

            sc = _score_from_conv(na, _conv_square_fft(na))
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = na.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose:
                    print(f"    {name} tau={tau:.4f} score={sc:.6f}")
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if int(res.status) in (0, 1) or no_improve >= 3:
                break

    # If time is left, keep polishing at a very small smoothing parameter.
    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 10.0:
        tau_final = max(0.0006, 0.30 * float(tau_schedule[-1][0]))
        no_improve = 0
        while time.monotonic() < t_end - 2.0:
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _fun_and_grad,
                    z,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    args=(tau_final, S, N, n),
                    options={
                        "maxiter": chunk,
                        "ftol": 1e-15,
                        "gtol": 1e-10,
                        "maxfun": max(20, 10 * chunk),
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                break
            if res.x is None:
                break
            nz = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if nz.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(nz)):
                break
            nz -= float(np.mean(nz))
            z = nz
            na = _softmax_fract(z, S)
            if na is None:
                break
            sc = _score_from_conv(na, _conv_square_fft(na))
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = na.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            if int(res.status) in (0, 1) or no_improve >= 3:
                break

    if verbose:
        print(f"  LBFGS {name}: final score={best_sc:.6f}")
    return best, float(best_sc)


def _init_chirp(n, seed):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - (n - 1) * 0.5
    a = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.cos(np.pi * t * t / float(n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = a + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return np.maximum(a, 0.0)


def _init_random(n, seed):
    return np.random.default_rng(seed).exponential(1.0, size=n)


def _make_candidate(a, label, idx, min_size=500, max_size=10000):
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size < min_size or a.size > max_size:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 1e-8:
        return None
    sc = _quick_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None
    return (sc, idx, label, a)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    total_budget = min(max(total_budget, 180.0), 2400.0)
    stop_time = t0 + total_budget - 45.0
    if stop_time < t0 + 90.0:
        stop_time = t0 + 90.0

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    final_candidates = []

    # Use the provided previous construction if it is safe and useful.
    prev = None
    try:
        prev_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if prev_arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(prev_arr)):
            prev_arr = np.maximum(prev_arr, 0.0)
            if float(np.sum(prev_arr)) > 1e-8:
                prev = prev_arr
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    if prev is not None:
        best_a, best_sc = _update_best(best_a, best_sc, prev, "prev")
        final_candidates = _add_final(final_candidates, best_a)

    K = 10

    # Low-dimensional shape search. This is the main global-shape optimizer.
    n_param = 2000
    param_budget = min(360.0, max(60.0, (stop_time - time.monotonic()) * 0.18))
    p_best = None
    if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 40.0:
        print(f"Phase A: parametric search n={n_param}, budget={param_budget:.0f}s")
        p_best, a_param, sc_param = _param_search(n_param, K, param_budget, stop_time, verbose=True)
        if p_best is None:
            p_best = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            p_best[0] = 0.5
        best_a, best_sc = _update_best(best_a, best_sc, a_param, "param_shape")
        final_candidates = _add_final(final_candidates, a_param)
    else:
        p_best = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        p_best[0] = 0.5

    # Build a candidate pool for full-dimensional polishing.
    hd_n = 7500
    S_hd = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * hd_n))

    large_items = []
    small_items = []
    idx = 0

    def _append_by_size(items_large, items_small, cand):
        if cand is not None:
            if cand[3].size >= 4000:
                items_large.append(cand)
            else:
                items_small.append(cand)
            return True
        return False

    # Parametric shape at high resolution.
    a = _param_shape(hd_n, K, p_best, S_hd)
    c = _make_candidate(a, "param_hd", idx)
    if c is not None:
        _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
        idx += 1

    # Pure half-singular baseline.
    p_u2 = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    p_u2[0] = 0.5
    a = _param_shape(hd_n, K, p_u2, S_hd)
    c = _make_candidate(a, "u2_hd", idx)
    if c is not None:
        _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
        idx += 1

    # A few asymmetric variants.
    p_asym1 = p_u2.copy()
    if K > 1:
        p_asym1[1] = -0.35
    if K > 2:
        p_asym1[2] = 0.25
    a = _param_shape(hd_n, K, p_asym1, S_hd)
    c = _make_candidate(a, "asym1_hd", idx)
    if c is not None:
        _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
        idx += 1

    p_asym2 = p_u2.copy()
    if K > 1:
        p_asym2[1] = 0.30
    if K > 2:
        p_asym2[2] = -0.20
    a = _param_shape(hd_n, K, p_asym2, S_hd)
    c = _make_candidate(a, "asym2_hd", idx)
    if c is not None:
        _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
        idx += 1

    # Other useful starting points.
    a = _init_chirp(hd_n, 11)
    c = _make_candidate(a, "chirp_hd", idx)
    if c is not None:
        _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
        idx += 1

    a = _init_random(hd_n, 12)
    c = _make_candidate(a, "random_hd", idx)
    if c is not None:
        _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
        idx += 1

    # Previous construction at native length, or interpolated to hd length.
    if prev is not None:
        if 800 <= prev.size <= 8000:
            c = _make_candidate(prev, "prev_native", idx)
            if c is not None:
                _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
                idx += 1
        a_prev_hd = _interp_seq(prev, hd_n)
        c = _make_candidate(a_prev_hd, "prev_interp", idx)
        if c is not None:
            _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
            idx += 1

    # Current best, if usable and not duplicate.
    c = _make_candidate(best_a, "current_best", idx, min_size=1000, max_size=8000)
    if c is not None:
        _append_by_size(large_items, small_items, c)
        idx += 1

    large_items.sort()
    small_items.sort()

    run_plan = []
    used_labels = set()

    if large_items:
        run_plan.append(large_items[0])
        used_labels.add(large_items[0][2])
    elif small_items:
        run_plan.append(small_items[0])
        used_labels.add(small_items[0][2])

    if small_items:
        for cand in small_items:
            if cand[2] not in used_labels:
                run_plan.append(cand)
                used_labels.add(cand[2])
                break

    for cand in large_items:
        if len(run_plan) >= 3:
            break
        if cand[2] not in used_labels:
            run_plan.append(cand)
            used_labels.add(cand[2])

    for cand in small_items:
        if len(run_plan) >= 3:
            break
        if cand[2] not in used_labels:
            run_plan.append(cand)
            used_labels.add(cand[2])

    # Full-dimensional polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 220.0 and run_plan:
        print(f"Phase B: full-dimensional polishing, {len(run_plan)} candidates")
        for i, cand in enumerate(run_plan):
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 160.0:
                break
            remaining = stop_time - time.monotonic()
            if i == 0:
                budget_run = min(1150.0, 0.45 * remaining)
            elif i == 1:
                budget_run = min(760.0, 0.38 * remaining)
            else:
                budget_run = min(520.0, 0.32 * remaining)
            if budget_run < 90.0:
                break
            res_a, res_sc = _run_lbfgs_seq(cand[3], budget_run, stop_time, name=cand[2])
            best_old_len = len(final_candidates)
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(best_a, best_sc, res_a, f"{cand[2]}_polish")
            final_candidates = _add_final(final_candidates, res_a)
            final_candidates = _add_final(final_candidates, best_a)

    # Final focused polish of the current best.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < stop_time - 140.0:
        remaining = stop_time - time.monotonic()
        budget_final = max(0.0, remaining - 70.0)
        if budget_final > 100.0:
            print(f"Phase C: final polish budget={budget_final:.0f}s")
            res_a, res_sc = _run_lbfgs_seq(
                best_a,
                budget_final,
                stop_time,
                tau_schedule=[(0.02, 0.35), (0.008, 0.35), (0.003, 0.30)],
                name="final_polish",
            )
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(best_a, best_sc, res_a, "final_polish")
            final_candidates = _add_final(final_candidates, res_a)

    final_candidates = _add_final(final_candidates, best_a)

    # Fall back to a valid construction if needed.
    if best_a is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)) or np.sum(best_a) <= 1e-8:
        best_a = np.ones(3000, dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = _quick_score(best_a)
        final_candidates = _add_final(final_candidates, best_a)

    # Exact re-evaluation of a few finalists using the official evaluator.
    seen = set()
    eval_count = 0
    for a_arr in final_candidates:
        if eval_count >= 6:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 15.0:
            break
        if a_arr is None or a_arr.size > 9000:
            continue
        key = _refine_key(a_arr)
        if key is None or key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        eval_count += 1
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a_arr])
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a_arr.copy()
                print(f"  exact evaluator improved: {best_sc:.6f}, n={best_a.size}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    for i in range(len(out)):
        x = out[i]
        if not np.isfinite(x) or x < 0.0:
            x = 0.0
        elif x > 1000.0:
            x = 1000.0
        out[i] = x

    print(f"=== FINAL: n={len(out)}, quick_score={best_sc:.6f} ===")
    return out
```
